HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000934/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_09fd8e7ad133d5d97551","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:3:4\",\"root_000934\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yiyecek, tek bir lokmadan öte, yoksulun geçim düzenindeki payını ifade eder; teşvik etmeyiş bu payın dolaşıma girmesini engeller.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0bb0461ae814db61eaf9","text":"2. **Low ground and abasement.** Evidence: `ح ض ض B002` low ground at a mountain-foot, `س ك ن B006` abasement/poverty; activated by `س م و B001` rising high in 107:0 and `س م و B005` raised designation. Structure: high divine naming contrasts with low social placement. Mechanism: the miskīn is not merely poor but held at the bottom while the actor seeks elevated name or display. Abductive move: I infer vertical social mapping; the packet supplies high/low and abasement branches. Reading change: “على طعام المسكين” becomes a scene of refused lifting from low ground.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003","root_000934/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_42c515377195f30945fe","text":"3. **Food as provisionary order, not morsel.** Evidence: `ط ع م B001` food/taste, `ط ع م B004` livelihood/legal share/revenue, `ط ع م B005` ripening/produce; activated by `د ي ن B003` debt/credit and `م ع ن B005` small available benefit. Structure: food sits between dīn-debt and maʿūn-benefit. Mechanism: feeding is part of an economy of due provision, shares, produce, and circulating small goods. Abductive move: I supply that “food” stands for access to livelihood channels; the packet supplies provision, legal share, debt, and lent benefit. Reading change: the ayah criticizes refusal to sustain the needy’s place in the provisioning system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B002","root_000726/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_451686657a8c69b28b7e","text":"2. **Urging is spatialized as lifting someone from a low, immobilized position.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ض ض/B002` is low ground at a mountain's foot; fixed `س ك ن/B006` is poverty, weakness, and abasement, while fixed `س ك ن/B010` is provision that enables staying. **Structural cue:** the locative image of low ground meets `على` and `طعام`, with the needy person as the endpoint of the construction. **Assigned role:** low ground maps the social position, and sustaining provision supplies the means of stable elevation rather than expulsion. **Abductive move:** this is exploratory because `B002` is a locative noun-image rather than the verbal sense of `يحض`; I infer the upward social arrow from its proximity to `على` and the abasement branch. **Reading change:** the absent exhortation becomes a refusal to organize a lift from debased precarity into sustainable standing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003","root_000934/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_47b4fe0550f8eb5835e7","text":"3. **The food reads as livelihood and an accountable share.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ط ع م/B001` is food that satisfies hunger, but fixed `ط ع م/B004` expands to provision, livelihood, revenue, and a legal share; nearby `د ي ن/B003` is debt, and 107:7's `م ع ن/B005` includes gifts, alms, household loans, and other available benefit. **Structural cue:** `طعام` is attached to `المسكين`, and the sequence runs from denial of liability to refusal of food to final withholding. **Assigned role:** the livelihood branch makes food part of a distributive system rather than a single morsel. **Abductive move:** the packet does not grammatically label the food a due; I infer entitlement from the possessive relation and the debt/withholding frame. **Reading change:** failure to urge is failure to recognize and mobilize the needy person's share in communal provision.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B002","root_000726/B006","root_000726/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4d0d13f9c6090a961996","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:3:2\",\"root_000334\",[\"B002\"]],[\"107:3:5\",\"root_000726\",[\"B006\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Alçak yerde kalma çağrışımı, yoksulluğu toplumsal olarak aşağıda ve hareketsiz tutulma şeklinde gösterir; eksik olan, geçimle desteklenen bir yükseliştir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B004","root_004706/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6e4062fd785685bc647e","text":"107:4-107:5 made the adjacency to prayer unexpectedly functional: because `س ك ن/B004` includes prayer as a source of rest, `المسكين` became the person to whom that rest fails to travel. 107:7 broadened the correction from one meal to a circulation system of small household benefits (`م ع ن/B005`), reinforcing `ط ع م/B004` as livelihood and share rather than isolated food.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B001","root_004706/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_750204518900ba6d34c7","text":"107:7 changed the food ayah most: `م ع ن B005` turned “food of the miskīn” into one node in a larger domestic circulation of small benefits. 107:5 also made the lack of `ح ض ض B001` feel like inertness: the problem is not only no food, but no movement of others toward food.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bb0d7f8820fc4c99081c","text":"1. **Failure to urge is a broken public mechanism.** Evidence: `ح ض ض B001` urging/inciting toward a thing, `ط ع م B002` feeding another, `س ك ن B006` poverty and humble abasement; activated by plural/social forms in 107:4-7. Structure: the ayah condemns not only non-feeding but non-mobilizing. Mechanism: food for the needy depends on communal pressure and exhortation; the denier blocks even the speech that would move resources. Abductive move: I supply the social-catalyst role of urging; the packet supplies urging, feeding, and needy abasement. Reading change: the ayah targets collapse of advocacy, not only private charity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B001","root_000726/B002","root_000726/B004","root_000726/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c942ea50b98f6256a549","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:3:5\",\"root_000726\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B004\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yoksulu doyurmak, onun barınma, dinlenme ve bulunduğu yerde güvenle kalma imkânını onarmaktır; ihtiyaç yalnız açlıkla sınırlı değildir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d829118f9961ad0b5cab","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:3:5\",\"root_000726\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yoksulun eksik huzuru, hemen ardından gelen namazı da sınar: ibadetin verdiği sükûnet başkasının hayatına taşınmıyorsa rahatlık kendi içinde tutulmuş olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B004","root_000879/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_da039b1a9971cd9ff6db","text":"4. **The needy person's missing rest becomes the test of prayer.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س ك ن/B004` is an object of rest and comfort and explicitly includes prayer among what the self rests in; 107:4-107:5 supply `ص ل و/B003`, prescribed prayer, and 107:7 supplies `م ن ع/B001`, withholding good. **Structural cue:** `المسكين` stands immediately before the warning to worshippers, whose sequence ends in withheld benefit. **Assigned role:** the comfort branch links the needy person's required stability to prayer's potential calming function. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies lexical overlap but not a stated transfer; I infer that worship's rest should issue in making social rest possible. **Reading change:** 107:3 becomes the hinge by which prayer is judged: ritual repose that never reaches the destabilized needy is exposed as blocked comfort.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B001","root_000934/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ec4c5317b530990e27d4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:3:2\",\"root_000334\",[\"B001\"]],[\"107:3:4\",\"root_000934\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Teşvik etmeyiş basit bir güçsüzlük değildir; kişi gücünü dışlama için kullanırken besleme yönünde toplumsal hareket oluşturmayı seçmez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fd5cb810a9dcf7a49317","text":"4. **Need as lack of settled rest.** Evidence: `س ك ن B001` cessation/stillness, `س ك ن B002` dwelling, `س ك ن B004` comfort/rest, `س ك ن B010` provision enabling staying. Structure: miskīn is built from a root whose branches include house, rest, and food that lets one remain. Mechanism: hunger destabilizes residence; feeding restores the capacity to stay and be calm. Abductive move: I infer that food repairs motion/agitation and homelessness; the packet supplies rest, dwelling, provision, and needy branches. Reading change: طعام المسكين reads as restoration of settled human life, not just caloric relief.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000334/B001","root_000477/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ff2333d4cf4a2bbe548f","text":"1. **The failure is a misallocation of social force.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ض ض/B001` is urging or inciting toward a thing; immediately prior `د ع ع/B001` is rough thrusting and forceful driving. **Structural cue:** the same identified agent is forceful in 107:2, but `لا` removes force precisely where exhortation toward food is required in 107:3. **Assigned role:** `حض` is beneficial propulsion, set against the harmful propulsion of `دع`. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the two opposite actions; I infer a common reserve of agency whose direction the subject chooses. **Reading change:** “does not urge” is not passive incapacity but force deliberately spent on exclusion rather than mobilized for provision.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
